                                                                                 更改財政年度結算日


On January 17, 2024, the Board announced that it has resolved to        於2024年1月17日，董事會公佈其已議決將本公司
change the financial year end date of the Company from June 30          的財政年度結算日由6月30日更改為12月31日，即
to December 31 with immediate effect. Accordingly, the Company          時生效。因此，本公司刊發涵蓋由2023年7月1日
published its audited consolidated financial statements covering a      起至2023年12月31日止六個月期間的經審核綜合
period of six months from July 1, 2023 to December 31, 2023. The        財務報表。本年度財務報告期的年報涵蓋2024年1
annual report for the current annual financial period covers a period   月1日起至2024年12月31日止十二個月期間（「截至
of twelve months from January 1, 2024 to December 31, 2024 (the         2024年12月31日止財政年度」）。
“fiscal year ended December 31, 2024”).

Given the foregoing, certain comparative information are for a          鑒於上文所述，部分比較資料乃自2023年7月1日起
period of six months from July 1, 2023 to December 31, 2023,            至2023年12月31日止六個月期間，因此可能不可
and hence may not be directly comparable. To enhance the                直接比較。為提高本年度財務業績的可比較性，本
comparability of the current year’s financial results, the Company      公司亦已將截至2023年12月31日止十二個月的未
has also included in this annual report the unaudited financial         經審計財務業績（披露於本公司截至2023年12月31
results of the Company for the twelve months ended December             日止六個月的年度報告及本公司截至2023年6月30
31, 2023, which are derived from the arithmetic combination of the      日止十二個月的年度報告中財務業績的算數合併，
financial results as disclosed in the annual report of the Company      經算數調整以剔除披露於本公司刊發的截至2022年
for the six months ended December 31, 2023 and the annual report        12月31日止六個月的中期報告的有關期間的前六個

財務表現摘要



NON-IFRS FINANCIAL MEASURES                                              非《國際財務報告準則》財務指標
In evaluating the business, MINISO considers and uses adjusted           於評估業務時，名創優品考慮並使用經調整淨利
net profit, adjusted EBITDA and adjusted basic and diluted net           潤、經調整EBITDA和經調整每股基本及攤薄淨盈利
earnings per share as supplemental measures to review and assess         作為審查及評估其經營業績的補充指標。呈列此等
its operating performance. The presentation of these non-IFRS            非《國際財務報告準則》財務指標並非旨在視為獨立
financial measures is not intended to be considered in isolation or      於或替代根據《國際財務報告準則》編製及呈列的財
as a substitute for the financial information prepared and presented     務資料。名創優品將經調整淨利潤定義為不包括以
in accordance with IFRS. MINISO defines adjusted net profit as           權益結算的股份支付開支的期內利潤。名創優品將
profit for the period excluding equity-settled share-based payment       經調整EBITDA界定為經調整淨利潤加折舊及攤銷、
expenses. MINISO defines adjusted EBITDA as adjusted net profit          財務成本及所得稅開支。名創優品以本公司權益股
plus depreciation and amortization, finance costs and income             東應佔經調整淨利潤除以按《國際財務報告準則》基
tax expense. MINISO computes adjusted basic and diluted net              準計算每股基本及攤薄盈利所用的股份數量計算經
earnings per share by dividing adjusted net profit attributable to the   調整每股基本及攤薄淨盈利。
equity shareholders of the Company by the number of Shares used
in the basic and diluted earnings per share calculation on an IFRS
basis.

MINISO presents these non-IFRS financial measures because they           名創優品呈報此等非《國際財務報告準則》財務指
are used by the management to evaluate its operating performance         標，因管理層以此等指標評估其經營業績及制定業
and formulate business plans. These non-IFRS financial measures          務計劃。此等非《國際財務報告準則》財務指標使管
enable the management to assess its operating results without            理層可在不考慮名創優品認為並非未來營運表現指
considering the impacts of the aforementioned non-cash and other         標的上述非現金及其他調整項目影響的情況下評估
adjustment items that MINISO does not consider to be indicative          其經營業績。因此，名創優品認為使用此等非《國際
of its operating performance in the future. Accordingly, MINISO          財務報告準則》財務指標為投資者及其他人士以與
believes that the use of these non-IFRS financial measures provides      管理層及董事會相同的方式理解及評估其經營業績
useful information to investors and others in understanding              提供了有用的資料。
and evaluating its operating results in the same manner as the
management and the Board.

These non-IFRS financial measures are not defined under IFRS             此等非《國際財務報告準則》財務指標未於《國際財
and are not presented in accordance with IFRS. These non-IFRS            務報告準則》中有所界定，亦未根據《國際財務報告
financial measures have limitations as analytical tools. One of the      準則》呈列。此等非《國際財務報告準則》財務指標
key limitations of using these non-IFRS financial measures is that       作為分析工具存在局限性。使用此等非《國際財務報
they do not reflect all items of income and expense that affect          告準則》財務指標的主要限制之一為其不能反映影
MINISO’s operations. Further, these non-IFRS financial measures          響名創優品營運的所有收入及開支項目。此外，此
may differ from the non-IFRS information used by other companies,        等非《國際財務報告準則》財務指標可能不同於其他
including peer companies, and therefore their comparability may be       公司（包括同行公司）使用的非《國際財務報告準則》
limited.                                                                 資料，因此其可比性可能有限。

These non-IFRS financial measures should not be considered in             此等非《國際財務報告準則》財務指標不應被獨立考
isolation or construed as alternatives to profit, basic and diluted       慮，或解釋為利潤、每股基本及攤薄盈利（如適用）
earnings per share, as applicable, or any other measures of               或任何其他業績指標的替代指標，或作為衡量名創
performance or as indicators of MINISO’s operating performance.           優品營運表現的指標。名創優品鼓勵投資者按以下
Investors are encouraged to review MINISO’s historical non-IFRS           最直接可比的《國際財務報告準則》指標審閱其歷史
financial measures in light of the most directly comparable IFRS          非《國際財務報告準則》財務指標。本文所呈列的非
measures, as shown below. The non-IFRS financial measures                 《國際財務報告準則》財務指標未必可與其他公司所
presented here may not be comparable to similarly titled measures         呈列的類似名稱指標作比較。其他公司對類似名稱
presented by other companies. Other companies may calculate               指標的計算方式可能不同，從而限制了有關指標在
similarly titled measures differently, limiting the usefulness of such    比較分析名創優品的數據時的有用性。名創優品鼓
measures when analyzing MINISO’s data comparatively. MINISO               勵閣下全面審閱其財務資料，而非依賴單一的財務
encourages you to review its financial information in its entirety and    指標。
not rely on a single financial measure.

our store network. Going forward, we expect to further grow our        現業務增長。

for-money proposition, we will continue to produce high-quality        IP設計為特色，持續產出優質IP作品，讓生活好物

fashionable and trendy.

GMV per store.

or capital expenditures. Our investment decisions with respect to      融產品的投資決定是以個案為基礎，並經適當和仔

and careful consideration of a number of factors, including, but not   經濟發展、預期投資條件、投資成本、投資期限
limited to, the market conditions, the economic developments, the      以及投資的預期收益和潛在損失。我們已經建立了

of the investment and the expected benefit and potential loss of the   報，同時減輕我們面臨的高投資風險。該等政策和
investment. We have established a set of internal control measures     措施由我們的高級管理層制定。
which allow us to achieve reasonable returns on our investment
while mitigating our exposure to high investment risks. These

consolidated trends. In addition, our results of operations, including   業績（包括利潤率）受到匯率波動的影響。我們的國
margins, are affected by the fluctuation in foreign exchange rates.      際業務主要以美元產生收入。一般而言，人民幣兌
Our international operations generate revenues primarily in U.S.         美元貶值對我們的經營業績產生積極影響，而人民
dollars. Generally, a weakening of RMB against U.S. dollar has a         幣兌美元升值則相反。我們並未使用任何衍生金融
positive effect on our results of operations, while a strengthening of   工具對沖有關風險。若我們需要將美元兌換成人民
RMB against U.S. dollar has the opposite effect. We have not used        幣用於營運，人民幣兌美元升值將對我們從兌換中

CHAIRMAN AND CHIEF EXECUTIVE OFFICER                                            主席兼首席執行官
Code provision C.2.1 of the Corporate Governance Code                           《企業管治守則》的守則條文第C.2.1條建議（但並無
recommends, but does not require, that the roles of chairman of the             規定）董事會主席與行政總裁的角色應有區分且不應
Board and chief executive officer should be separate and should                 由同一人兼任。
not be performed by the same individual.

The Company deviates from this code provision as we do not                      本公司偏離該守則條文，因為我們並無單獨的主席
have a separate chairman and chief executive officer and Mr. Ye                 與首席執行官，葉先生現兼任本公司這兩個職務。
currently performs these two roles of the Company. Mr. Ye is our                葉先生為我們的創始人，深諳我們的業務營運及管
founder and has extensive experience in our business operations                 理。董事會認為，主席與首席執行官由同一人士兼
and management. The Board believes that vesting the roles of                    任可確保本集團內部的一致領導，並使本集團的整
both chairperson and chief executive officer in the same person                 體戰略規劃更為有效及高效。董事會認為現時的安
has the benefit of ensuring consistent leadership within our Group              排並不會損害權力及權責的平衡，而此結構將確保
and enables more effective and efficient overall strategic planning             本公司迅速及有效地作出及執行決定。董事會將在
for our Group. The Board considers that the balance of power and                考慮本集團的整體情況後，適時繼續審議及考慮區
authority for the present arrangement will not be impaired and                  分董事會主席與本公司首席執行官的職責。
this structure will enable our Company to make and implement
decisions promptly and effectively. The Board will continue to
review and consider splitting the roles of chairman of the Board
and the chief executive officer of our Company if and when it is
appropriate taking into account the circumstances of the Group as
a whole.

BOARD MEETINGS                                                          董事會會議
The Company adopts the practice of holding Board meetings               本公司採納定期舉行董事會會議的慣例，每年至少
regularly, at least four times a year, and at approximately quarterly   舉行四次會議，大約每季舉行一次。本公司就所有
intervals. Notices of not less than 14 days are given for all regular   定期董事會會議發出不少於14日的通知，以向全體
Board meetings to provide all Directors with an opportunity to          董事提供機會出席定期會議並將有關事項納入議程。
attend and include matters in the agenda for a regular meeting.

For other Board meetings and Board Committee meetings,                  就其他董事會會議及董事委員會會議而言，本公司
reasonable notice is generally given by the Company. The agenda         一般會發出合理通知。議程及隨附會議文件於董事
and accompanying meeting papers are provided to the Directors           會會議或董事委員會會議舉行前至少三天提供予董
or Board Committee members at least three days before the Board         事或董事委員會成員，以確保董事有充足時間審閱
meetings or Board Committee meetings to ensure that the Directors       有關文件及充分準備出席董事會會議或董事委員會
have sufficient time to review the papers and be adequately             會議。倘董事或董事委員會成員未能出席會議，則
prepared for the Board meetings or Board Committee meetings.            彼等會獲悉將予討論的事宜，並有機會於會議召開
When Directors or Board Committee members are unable to attend          前知會主席有關彼等的意見。會議紀錄由聯席公司
a meeting, they will be advised of the matters to be discussed and      秘書保存，副本將於全體董事間傳閱，以供參考及
given an opportunity to make their views known to the chairman          紀錄。
prior to the meeting. Minutes of meetings are kept by the joint
company secretaries with copies circulated to all Directors for

Minutes of the Board meetings and Board Committee meetings are          董事會會議及董事委員會會議的會議記錄會充足詳
recorded in sufficient detail on the matters considered by the Board    盡記錄董事會及董事委員會所考慮的事項及所達致
and the Board Committees and the decisions reached, including           的決定，包括董事提出的任何關注。各董事會會議
any concerns raised by the Directors. Draft minutes of each Board       及董事委員會會議的會議記錄草擬本將於會議舉行
meeting and Board Committee meeting are sent to the Directors           當日後的合理時間內寄送至各董事，以供彼等提出
for comments within a reasonable time after the date on which the       意見。董事會會議記錄可供全體董事查閱。
meeting is held. The minutes of the Board meetings are open for
inspection by all Directors.

RESPONSIBILITIES AND DELEGATION BY                                       董事會職責及授權
THE BOARD
The Board is the primary decision-making body of the Company             董事會為本公司的主要決策機構，負責監督本集團
and is responsible for overseeing the Group’s businesses,                的業務、戰略決策及表現，並透過指導及監督本公
strategic decisions and performance and is collectively responsible      司事務，共同負責促進本公司的成功。董事會客觀
for promoting the success of the Company by directing and                作出符合本公司利益的決策。全體董事（包括獨立非
supervising its affairs. The Board makes decisions objectively in        執行董事）為董事會帶來廣泛而寶貴的業務經驗、知
the interests of the Company. All Directors, including independent       識及專業精神，使其高效及有效地運作。
non-executive Directors, have brought a wide spectrum of valuable
business experience, knowledge and professionalism to the Board
for its efficient and effective functioning.

The day-to-day management, administration and operation of               本集團的日常管理、行政及營運乃授權予高級管理
the Group are delegated to the senior management. The Board              層負責。董事會定期檢討所委派的職能及職責。高
regularly reviews the functions and responsibilities delegated. The      級管理層於訂立任何重大交易前須取得董事會批准。
senior management needs to obtain approval from the Board prior
to entering into of any significant transactions.

The Board reserves the right to make decisions on all substantial        董事會保留就本公司所有重大事宜作出決策的權
affairs of the Company, including: to approve and oversee all policy     利，包括：批准及監督所有政策及事宜、整體戰略
and matters, overall strategy and budgets, internal control and risk     及預算、內部監控及風險管理系統、重大交易（尤其
management systems, material transactions (in particular those that      是可能涉及利益衝突的交易）、財務資料、董事的委
may involve conflict of interests), financial information, appointment   任及其他主要財務及營運事宜。
of Directors and other major financial and operational issues.

The Company has adopted a policy to ensure independent views             本公司已採納一項政策，以確保董事會能獲得獨立
and inputs are available to the Board. The Directors may, at the         觀點及意見。董事可尋求其認為必要的獨立專業建
Company’s expense, seek independent professional advice, views           議、觀點及意見以履行其職責，及於根據有關政策
and input as considered necessary to fulfill their responsibilities      作出履行職責的決定時行使獨立判斷，費用由本公
and in exercising independent judgement when making decisions            司承擔。董事會將每年檢討有關機制的實施及成
in performing their duties according to such policy. The Board will      效。董事會已檢討報告期董事會獨立機制的實施及
review the implementation and effectiveness of such mechanism            成效，並認為其行之有效。
on an annual basis. The Board has reviewed the implementation

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE FUNCTION                                          企業管治職能
The Board confirmed that corporate governance is a collective          董事會確認，企業管治為董事的共同責任，彼等的
responsibility of the Directors and their corporate governance         企業管治職能包括：
functions include:

(a)   to review and monitor the Company’s policies and practices       (a)   檢討及監察本公司在遵守法律及監管規定方面
      in complying with legal and regulatory requirements;                   的政策及常規；

(b)   to review and monitor the training and continuous professional   (b)   檢討及監察董事及本公司高級管理層的培訓及
      development of the Directors and senior management of the              持續專業發展；
      Company;

(c)   to develop, review and monitor the code of conduct and           (c)   制定、檢討及監察僱員及董事適用的操守準則
      compliance manual applicable to employees and the                      及合規手冊；
      Directors;

(d)   to develop and review the Company’s policies and practices       (d)   制定及檢討本公司的企業管治政策及常規，並
      on corporate governance, and to make recommendations and               向董事會提出建議及匯報相關事宜；
      report related issues to the Board;

(e)   to review the Company’s compliance with the Corporate            (e)   檢討本公司遵守《企業管治守則》的情況及在
      Governance Code and disclosure in the Corporate                        企業管治報告內的披露；及
      Governance Report; and

(f)   to review and monitor the Company’s compliance with the          (f)   檢討及監察本公司遵守公司報告政策的情況。
      Company’s reporting policies.

eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business objectives, and            虧損提供合理而非絕對的保證。
can only provide reasonable and not absolute assurance against
material misstatement or loss.

amendments of which took effect upon the Listing. Since the            效。自上市起，2020年股份激勵計劃受《上市規則》

OPINION                                                                 意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of MINISO         我們已審計列載於第98至237頁的名創優品集團控
Group Holding Limited (“the Company”) and its subsidiaries              股有限公司（「貴公司」）及其附屬公司（「貴集團」）的
(“the Group”) set out on pages 98 to 237, which comprise the            綜合財務報表，此財務報表包括於2024年12月31
consolidated statement of financial position as at December 31,         日的綜合財務狀況表、截至該日止年度的綜合損益
2024, the consolidated statement of profit or loss, the consolidated    表、綜合損益及其他全面收益表、綜合權益變動表
statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive income, the         及綜合現金流量表以及綜合財務報表附註，包括重
consolidated statement of changes in equity and the consolidated        大會計政策概要。

Assessment of impairment of long-term assets of self-operated stores
評估直營店的長期資產減值

Refer to Notes 11 and 12 to the consolidated financial statements and the accounting policies on pages 127 and 128.
請參閱綜合財務報表附註11及12及第127及128頁的會計政策。

The Key Audit Matter                                            How the matter was addressed in our audit
關鍵審計事項                                                          我們的審計如何處理該事項

We identified the assessment of impairment of property,
plant and equipment and right-of-use assets related to
certain self-operated stores as a key audit matter. Complex
auditor judgement was required in evaluating the market
rentals used by the Group in estimating the self-operated
stores’ fair value less costs of disposal. Specifically,
specialized skills and knowledge were needed to evaluate
the reasonableness of comparable transactions used by
the Group to estimate the market rentals.
我們將與若干直營店相關的物業、廠房及設備及使用權資產
的減值評估列為一項關鍵審計事項。在評估貴集團用於估算
自營店公平價值減去處置成本時所使用的市場租金時，需要
複雜的審計判斷。具體來說，需要專門的技能和知識來評估
貴集團用於估算市場租金的可比交易的合理性。

          (i)   Subsidiaries and non-controlling interests                           (i)    附屬公司及非控股權益

                substantive rights (held by the Group and other                             慮實質性權利（由本集團和其他各
                parties) are considered.                                                    方持有）。

                 resulting gain or loss is recognized in profit or loss.                        生收益或虧損於損益中確認。於前
                 Any interest retained in that former subsidiary is                             附屬公司保留的任何權益在喪失控
                 measured at fair value when control is lost.                                   制權時按公允價值計量。

                      An interest in an associate is accounted for                            對聯營公司的權益採用權益法核
                      using the equity method. Under the equity                               算。根據權益法，投資最初按成本
                      method, the investment is initially recorded at                         入賬，並就本集團應得的被投資公
                      cost, adjusted for any excess of the Group’s                            司可辨認淨資產收購日的公允價值
                      share of the acquisition-date fair values of the                        超出投資成本的部分（如有）進行
                      investee’s identifiable net assets over the cost of                     調整。投資成本包括購買價、直接
                      the investment (if any). The cost of the investment                     產生自獲得投資的其他成本及構成
                      includes purchase price, other costs directly                           本集團權益投資一部分的對聯營公
                      attributable to the acquisition of the investment,                      司或合營企業的任何直接投資。此
                      and any direct investment into the associate                            後，該投資會就本集團應佔被投資
                      or joint venture that forms part of the Group’s                         公司資產淨值於收購後的變動及與

          At inception of a contract, the Group assesses whether                      在合約開始時，本集團會評估該合約是

          time in exchange for consideration. Control is conveyed                     如客戶既有權指示已確定資產的使用，
          where the customer has both the right to direct the use                     又有權從該使用中獲得絕大部分經濟利
          of the identified asset and to obtain substantially all of                  益，則控制權被轉移。
          the economic benefits from that use.

          set out in paragraph 46B of IFRS 16 Leases . In such                         利用實際權宜法，不評估租賃減免是否

                      The Group recognizes a loss allowance for                              本集團就以攤銷成本計量的金融資
                      expected credit losses (ECLs) on financial assets                      產（包括現金及現金等價物、受限
                      measured at amortized cost (including cash and                         制現金、定期存款、貿易及其他應

                      trade and other receivables).                                          撥備。

                      are not subject to the ECL assessment.                                 資）無需進行預期信貸虧損評估。

                     Measurement of ECLs                                                     預期信貸虧損的計量
                     ECLs are a probability-weighted estimate of                             預期信貸虧損是以概率加權估計的
                     credit losses. Credit losses are measured as the                        信貸虧損。信貸虧損按所有預期現
                     present value of all expected cash shortfalls (i.e.                     金差額（即根據合約應付本集團的
                     the difference between the cash flows due to the                        現金流與本集團預期收取現金流之
                     Group in accordance with the contract and the                           間的差額）的現值計量。
                     cash flows that the Group expects to receive).

                      The expected cash shortfalls are discounted using                      如折現的影響重大，則預期現金差
                      the following discount rates where the effect of                       額將按以下折現率折現：
                      discounting is material:

                      •      fixed-rate financial assets and trade and                       •   固定利率金融資產和貿易及
                             other receivables: effective interest rate                          其他應收款項：初始確認時
                             determined at initial recognition or an                             確定的實際利率或其近似值。
                             approximation thereof.

                      The maximum period considered when estimating                          估計預期信貸虧損時所考慮的最長
                      ECLs is the maximum contractual period over                            期限為本集團面臨信貸風險的最長
                      which the Group is exposed to credit risk.                             合約期。

                      In measuring ECLs, the Group takes into account                        在計量預期信貸虧損時，本集團考
                      reasonable and supportable information that                            慮合理及有理據而無需付出過多的
                      is available without undue cost or effort. This                        成本或努力即可獲得的資料，包括
                      includes information about past events, current                        過往事件、當前狀況及未來經濟狀
                      conditions and forecasts of future economic                            況預測等資料。
                      conditions.

               and low value consumables to be consumed in the                         中消耗的低價消耗品。
               ordinary course of business.

               Inventories are carried at the lower of cost and net                    存貨按成本和可變現淨值二者中的較低
               realizable value.                                                       者列賬。

               Cost of inventories is calculated using the weighted                    存貨成本採用加權平均法計算。
               average method.

               Net realizable value is the estimated selling price in the              可變現淨值指日常業務過程中的估計售
               ordinary course of business less the estimated costs                    價扣除進行銷售必要的估計成本。
               necessary to make the sale.

               When inventories are sold, the carrying amount of those                 售出存貨時，該等存貨的賬面金額在確
               inventories is recognized as an expense in the period in                認相關收入的期間確認為開支。
               which the related revenue is recognized.

          Current tax is the expected tax payable on the taxable                     即期稅項為按期間應課稅收入，根據於

          previous periods. The amount of current tax payable is                     稅項的金額是對預期支付或收到的稅款
          the best estimate of the tax amount expected to be paid                    的最佳估計，反映了與所得稅有關的不
          or received that reflects uncertainty related to income                    確定性（如有）。
          taxes, if any.

          Deferred tax assets and liabilities arise from deductible                  遞延稅項資產及負債分別由可抵扣和應
          and taxable temporary differences respectively, being                      課稅暫時性差異產生，即資產及負債就

    (s)   Provisions and contingent liabilities                                (s)    撥備及或有負債
          Provisions are recognized when the Group has a legal                        當本集團有因過往事件而產生的法律或
          or constructive obligation arising as a result of a past                    推定義務，很可能需要產生經濟利益流

          will be required to settle the obligation and a reliable                    時，確認撥備。倘貨幣的時間價值屬重
          estimate can be made. Where the time value of money                         大時，撥備乃按預計用於清償債務支出
          is material, provisions are stated at the present value of                  的現值列示。
          the expenditure expected to settle the obligation.

          Where it is not probable that an outflow of economic                        倘需要經濟利益流出的可能性不大，或
          benefits will be required, or the amount cannot be                          者數額不能可靠估計，該義務應作為或
          estimated reliably, the obligation is disclosed as a                        有負債披露，除非經濟利益流出的可能
          contingent liability, unless the probability of outflow                     性極小。只有一項或多項未來事件的
          of economic benefits is remote. Possible obligations,                       發生或不發生才能確認其存在的潛在義
          whose existence will only be confirmed by the                               務，亦作為或有負債披露，除非經濟利
          occurrence or non-occurrence of one or more future                          益流出的可能性極小。
          events are also disclosed as contingent liabilities unless
          the probability of outflow of economic benefits is
          remote.

    (d)   Unrecognized deferred tax assets                                         (d)    未確認的遞延稅項資產

 22 CAPITAL AND RESERVES (continued)                                             22 資本及儲備（續）
        (b)    Nature and purposes of reserves                                     (b)   儲備的性質及目的
               (i)     Merger reserve                                                    (i)     合併儲備
                       The merger reserve mainly represents the                                  合併儲備主要指同一控制下企業合
                       difference between the consideration paid and                             併所支付的對價與取得的實收資本
                       the paid-in capital acquired arising from business                        之間的差額。
                       combinations involving entities under common
                       control.

               (ii)    Translation reserve                                               (ii)    換算儲備
                       The exchange reserve comprises all foreign                                換算儲備包括換算國外業務財務報
                       exchange differences arising from the translation                         表產生的所有外匯差額。
                       of the financial statements of foreign operations.

               (iii)   Share-based payment reserve                                       (iii)   以股份為基礎的付款儲備
                       The share-based payment reserve represents the                            以股份為基礎的付款儲備是指已根
                       portion of the grant date fair value of restricted                        據附註2(q)(iii)中就以股份為基礎的
                       shares, share options and restricted share units                          付款所採納的會計政策確認的授予
                       granted to the key management personnel and                               本集團關鍵管理人員及僱員的受限
                       employees of the Group that has been recognized                           制股份、購股權及受限制股份單位
                       in accordance with the accounting policy adopted                          的授予日公允價值部分。
                       for share-based payments in Note 2(q)(iii).

               (iv)    PRC statutory reserve                                             (iv)    中國法定公積金
                       PRC statutory reserves are established in                                 中國法定公積金乃根據中國《公司
                       accordance with the PRC Company Law and the                               法》和在中國成立的附屬公司的組
                       Articles of Association of the subsidiaries which                         織章程細則而設立。如附屬公司為
                       are established in the PRC. The subsidiaries                              外商獨資企業或內資獨資企業，則
                       being wholly foreign-owned enterprise or wholly                           必須將其淨利潤的至少10%分配
                       domestic-owned enterprises, are required to                               至法定盈餘公積金。轉撥至法定盈
                       allocate at least 10% of its net profits to a statutory                   餘公積金必須在向權益股東分派股
                       surplus reserve. The transfer to this reserve must                        息之前作出。
                       be made before distribution of dividends to equity
                       shareholders can be made.

                       PRC statutory reserve can be used to make                                 中國法定公積金可用於彌補以往年
                       good previous years’ losses, if any, and may be                           度的虧損（如有），及可按其現有持

        Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency             本集團在日常業務過程中承受信貸、流動性、
        risks arises in the normal course of the Group’s business.            利率及貨幣風險。下文呈列本集團所承受的上
        The Group’s exposure to these risks and the financial risk            述風險及本集團為管理該等風險所採用的財務
        management policies and practices used by the Group to                風險管理政策及實務。
        manage these risks are described below.

        (a)    Credit risk                                                    (a)   信貸風險
               Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty will              信貸風險指交易對手未履行其合約責任
               default on its contractual obligations resulting in a                而導致本集團產生財務虧損的風險。本
               financial loss to the Group. The Group’s credit risk is              集團的信貸風險主要歸因於貿易及其他
               primarily attributable to trade and other receivables.               應收款項。由於交易對手為本集團認為
               The Group’s exposure to credit risk arising from cash                信貸風險低且具有高信貸質量的銀行及
               and cash equivalents, restricted cash and term deposits              金融機構，因此本集團承擔來自現金及
               is limited because the counterparties are banks and                  現金等價物、受限制現金及定期存款的
               financial institutions with high-credit-quality, for which           信貸風險有限。
               the Group considers having low credit risk.

               Trade receivables                                                    貿易應收款項
               The Group’s trade receivables mainly derive from sales               本集團的貿易應收款項主要來自於向代
               of goods to distributors and franchisees. The Group’s                理商及加盟商銷售貨物。本集團面臨的
               exposure to credit risk is influenced mainly by the                  信貸風險主要受每個客戶的個別特徵（而
               individual characteristics of each customer rather than              非客戶經營所在的行業或國家）影響，
               the industry or country in which the customers operate               因此，當本集團與個別客戶有大額往來
               and therefore significant concentrations of credit risk              時，即面對信貸風險高度集中的情況。
               primarily arise when the Group has significant exposure              於2023年及2024年12月31日，貿易應
               to individual customers. At December 31, 2023 and                    收款項總額的40%及35%分別來自本集
               2024, 40% and 35% of the total trade receivables were                團的五大債務人。
               due from the Group’s five largest debtors, respectively.

               Individual credit evaluations are performed on all                   我們會對所有要求獲得超出若干金額的
               customers requiring credit over a certain amount. These              信貸的客戶進行個別信貸評估。該等評
               evaluations focus on the customer’s history of making                估針對客戶到期還款記錄及現時還款能
               payments when due and current ability to pay and take                力，並考慮客戶的具體信息以及與客戶
               into account information specific to the customer as well            經營所在經濟環境有關的信息。
               as pertaining to the economic environment in which the
               customer operates.

               Trade receivables relating to certain sales of fixtures              與向加盟商銷售若干裝修材料有關的貿
               to franchisees are collected by instalments within                   易應收款項於18至38個月期間內分期收
               the periods ranging from 18 to 38 months. All other                  取。所有其他貿易應收款項於自開票日
               trade receivables are due within 30 to 180 days from                 期起30至180天內到期。擁有逾期6個月
               the date of billing. Debtors with balances that are                  以上結餘的債務人被要求在獲授予任何
               more than 6 months past due are requested to settle                  進一步信貸之前結清所有的未清餘額。
               all outstanding balances before any further credit is                通常情況下，本集團不會從客戶那裏獲
               granted. Normally, the Group does not obtain collateral              得抵押品。
               from customers.

  (c)   Interest rate risk                                                        (c)    利率風險
        Interest rate risk is the risk that the fair value or future                     利率風險是指金融工具的公允價值或未
        cash flows of a financial instrument will fluctuate                              來現金流量因市場利率變動而波動的風
        because of changes in market interest rates. The Group                           險。於各報告期末，本集團並無將任何
        does not account for any fixed-rate financial instruments                        固定利率金融工具按公允價值計入損
        at fair value through profit or loss at the end of each                          益。因此，按固定利率計息的金融工具
        reporting periods. Therefore, interest-bearing financial                         不會使本集團面臨公允價值利率風險。
        instruments at fixed rates do not expose the Group to                            本集團的利率風險主要來自受限制現金
        fair value interest rate risk. The Group’s interest rate risk                    及以浮動利率計息的銀行存款，這使本
        arises primarily from restricted cash and cash at bank                           集團面臨現金流量利率風險。本集團根
        at variable rates, which exposes the Group to cash flow                          據目前的市場情況釐定固定利率和浮動
        interest rate risk. The Group determines the appropriate                         利率計息工具的適當權重，並定期進行
        weightings of the fixed and floating rate interest-bearing                       審查和監測，以實現固定利率和浮動利
        instruments based on the current market conditions and                           率風險的適當組合。本集團並無訂立金
        performs regular reviews and monitoring to achieve an                            融衍生工具對沖其利率風險。
        appropriate mix of fixed and floating rate exposure. The
        Group does not enter into financial derivatives to hedge
        interest rate risk.
